# KTM family (KTM, Husqvarna, GasGas): aftermarket parts research catalog

Research candidates gathered from public web sources (manufacturer pages, dealers, retailers, magazine tests, testers, forums). Each product keeps its source and a confidence: high = the manufacturer lists the part number and fitment; medium = a dealer or major retailer does; unverified = inferred or title-only. Nothing here is verified evidence, nothing is shown to riders yet, and no fitment, price or performance claim should be repeated without checking the source. Performance claims are the maker's or tester's, with their conditions.

Files: ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json, ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json, ktm-exhaust-intake-fuel.json, ktm-oem-accessories.json, ktm-rider-feedback.json. Built by scripts/aftermarket/build_catalog.py.

Counts: 112 products, 139 part numbers, 54 rider/tester findings, 11 project-bike builds, 217 of 224 in-scope bike-years with at least one mapped product.

## Project-bike builds

### [2026 KTM 450 SX-F Garage Build (Racer X Films / Keefer)](https://racerxonline.com/2026/09/16/2026-ktm-450-sx-f-garage-build) — KTM 450 SX-F 2026, Racer X (also posted on Keefer Inc Testing 2026-09-21, video only) 2026-09-16

- exhaust_flange: Twisted Development Exhaust flange — Not stated in text
- triple_clamps: Ride Engineering 23.5mm offset triple clamps — Not stated in text
- linkage: Ride Engineering Linkage — Not stated in text
- brakes: Ride Engineering Front brake rotor kit — Not stated in text
- suspension: WP Suspension / REP Suspension AER fork revalved; stock shock revalved with bladder — Chassis/suspension stage
- tires: Dunlop MX34 90/100-21 front, 110/100-18 rear — Not stated
- handlebar: Renthal 661 Fatbar; Half Waffle Medium grips — Not stated
- controls: ASV S1 single-pivot clutch and brake levers — Not stated
- throttle: Pro Circuit Throttle tube — Not stated
- drivetrain: DID 520 Gold ERT3 chain — Not stated
- graphics: DeCal Works Shroud kit, pre-print numbers — Cosmetic

### [MXA Race Test: Twisted Development's KTM 250SXF Race Build](https://motocrossactionmag.com/mxa-race-test-twisted-developments-ktm-250sxf-race-build/) — KTM 250 SX-F Factory Edition 2025, Motocross Action (MXA) 2025-09-09

- engine: Twisted Development Stage 1 head, stiffer valve springs, new air boot, second injector, Vortex map — More power; found: Pulled hard and wheelied easily; sounded like a factory 250. $5,066.09 parts and labor.
- fuel: ETS MX18 — Race fuel, mapped; found: Less pungent smell than MX21
- suspension: REP Suspension Revalved WP XACT Pro fork and shock with hidden bladder — Pro-level stiffness and comfort; found: Good for Ezra; firm for Mosiman but suited rough Mammoth. $1,092.82.
- handlebar: Fasst Company Flexx bars — Comfort; found: Comfort without vague turn-in
- seat: Guts Racing Gripper seat; RJ wing cover — Grip; found: Wing cover helped more on the tall KTM seat
- gearing: unspecified 53T rear sprocket — Altitude pulling power; found: Helped at Mammoth; ran through gears quickly
- tires: Dunlop MX34 — Tires
- air_filter: Twin Air Filters — Spares
- graphics: Decal Works Graphics — Cosmetic

### [Racer X Films: 2025 KTM 450 SX-F Garage Build](https://racerxonline.com/2024/09/17/racer-x-films-2025-ktm-450-sx-f-garage-build) — KTM 450 SX-F 2025, Racer X 2024-09-17

- exhaust: FMF Racing Factory 4.1 full system (insert in) — Power/torque
- ecu: XPR Motorsports Vortex ECU, custom map — Power/torque
- triple_clamps: WP Suspension Factory split triple clamp — Comfort
- exhaust_flange: Twisted Development Exhaust flange — Torque
- suspension_conversion: REP Suspension KYB fork 25mm cartridge kit; WP-valved TRAX shock; linkage system; top engine hangers — Comfort
- handlebar: Renthal 672 Fatbar cut to 802mm; Half Waffle Soft grips — Ergonomics
- clutch: Hinson Racing Complete clutch kit — Clutch
- tires: Dunlop MX34 120/80-19 rear, 80/100-21 front — Tires
- fuel: VP Racing Fuels MR Pro6-HT — Fuel
- wheels: DID Ace Orange wheelset; ERT3 Gold chain — Wheels/drive
- throttle: Zero Resistance Throttle Throttle tube (black cam) — Throttle feel

### [Keefer personal 2024 KTM 350 SX-F race build](https://www.keeferinctesting.com/race-test-the-mods-that-i-have-done-to-my-personal-ktm-350-sx-f-machine/) — KTM 350 SX-F 2024, Keefer Inc Testing 2023-08-15

- handlebar: Pro Taper Husqvarna Stock EVO bend, cut to 802mm — Vibration/fatigue; found: Less vibration and soreness; still more sore than on a Yamaha
- throttle: ZRT Milled throttle tube — Arm fatigue; found: Smoother, lighter pull
- exhaust: FMF Racing 4.1 titanium full system — Power, weight; found: More mid and top; almost 2 lb lighter; packing every 10-12 hours
- suspension: REP Suspension CV fork setting, Trax shock, linkage — Comfort/hold-up; found: Most comfortable WP Pro setup he has run; needs a shock spring change
- ecu: XPR Motorsports Vortex map for FMF and MX18 — Power, throttle feel; found: More torque and over-rev, clean running; about $1,000
- fuel: ETS MX18 — Response; found: Better low and mid than pump or VP T4; must be mapped

### [Keefer: 2024 KTM 450 SX-F Baseline Settings/Mods](https://www.keeferinctesting.com/2024-ktm-450-sx-f-baseline-settings-mods/) — KTM 450 SX-F 2024, Keefer Inc Testing 2023-09-08

- triple_clamps: Ride Engineering 23.5mm split clamps — Front feel, rut entry; found: Calmer off-throttle, less firm on braking bumps
- exhaust: FMF Racing System for 2023+ 450 SX-F (insert in) — Mid/top pull; found: More mid/top; slightly less low end
- handlebar: Pro Taper / FASST Co Husqvarna Stock EVO at 800-803mm; FLEXX 12-degree alternative — Vibration; found: Less vibration
- ecu: Vortex / XPR Vortex ECU, XPR map — Usable power; found: Better second gear, linear low end, rear traction
- suspension_conversion: WP XACT Pro 6500 cartridge kit — Coil fork feel; found: More front feel than AER; end-stroke damping off; setting-dependent harshness
- tires: Dunlop MX34 (110 rear recommended) — Traction

### [Parts That We Have Tried, That Work (2023 GasGas MC450)](https://www.keeferinctesting.com/parts-that-we-have-tried-that-work-2023-gas-gas-mc450/) — GasGas MC 450F 2023, Keefer Inc Testing 2023-02-11

- ecu: Twisted Development Vortex mapping + exhaust flange — Low-end torque; found: More torque in soft corners, better third-gear roll-on
- suspension_conversion: Technical Touch KYB spring conversion (Shock Therapy valving) — Replace AER48; found: Less harsh, more consistent
- triple_clamps: X-Trig ROCS clamps with PHDS mounts (22mm) — Vibration, front feel; found: Less hand vibration, more front positivity
- brakes: Moto-Master Factory Billet caliper + 270mm rotor — Braking control; found: Linear, less grabby, fewer washouts

### [Top Five 2023 Husqvarna FC350 Modifications](https://www.keeferinctesting.com/top-five-2023-husqvarna-fc350-modifications/) — Husqvarna FC 350 2023, Keefer Inc Testing 2022-10-10

- suspension_conversion: Enzo Racing KYB spring conversion + revalved shock — Comfort for a 230 lb rider; found: Better lean grip, plusher; run at full height with KTM linkage
- ecu: Twisted Development Vortex map — Fix lean spots and decel popping; found: More power, traction
- exhaust: FMF Racing 4.1 slip-on — Power; found: Stronger bottom pull (conflicts with Keefer's separate slip-on comparison)
- throttle: ZRT Throttle tube — Smoothness; found: Smoother connection
- handlebar: Pro Taper Fuzion Race Team 996 Replica (crossbar removed) — Flex/feel; found: Rider preference
- axle_blocks: Works Connection Elite axle blocks — Free-floating left block; found: Easier chain adjustment; no traction gain noticed
- launch_device: Works Connection Pro Launch at 130mm — Starts; found: Front stays down; reliable

### [MXA Builds a 2022 GasGas MC 450F Factory Edition](https://motocrossactionmag.com/we-build-mxas-2022-gasgas-mc-450f-project-racer/) — GasGas MC 450F 2022, Motocross Action (MXA) 2021-12-17

- suspension_conversion: Coppersmith Aero/Hybrid fork kit — Coil feel with air bottoming; found: Coil-like start, resisted bottoming; 4.5 N/mm, 14 out, 55 psi; $895 DIY
- triple_clamps: X-Trig ROCS split clamps + Flex Fit mounts — Steering, vibration; found: Fixed stock GasGas oversteer-in/understeer-out; less vibration. $899 + $190
- suspension_shock: MX-Tech National shock — Tunable rear; found: Highly adjustable, more than 1 lb lighter, clickers take time to learn; $2,400
- wheels: NacStar Works wheelset — Lighter/stronger; found: No problems on race day; $599
- ecu: Vortex / Twisted Development X10 with two TD maps + KTM multi-switch — Power and engine-braking maps; found: Map 1 aggressive, Map 2 less engine braking
- brakes: Brembo (KTM PowerParts) Factory SXS front caliper — Braking; found: Strong, progressive past 50% lever; too strong at low speed; $1,000
- engine_mounts: FCP Aluminum head stays; titanium footpeg pins — Handling; found: Plusher feel; $400
- handlebar: ODI Podium Flight MX bar with Emig Pro V2 grips (626) — Replace stock Neken
- seat: Motoseat Five-rib cover — Grip
- footpegs: Raptor Apex titanium footpegs — Footpegs
- tires: Hoosier IMX25S front / IMX25 C100 rear (120-19) — Tires; found: Ran 120 because Hoosier 110s measure closer to 100
- drivetrain: Pro-X / TM Designworks Sprockets; chain guide and buffer pads — Drivetrain
- cosmetic: Nihilo Concepts / Decal Works CNC covers, grip tape; graphics — Finish

### [Project Build: 2021 GASGAS MC 450F (Vital MX)](https://www.vitalmx.com/features/Project-Build-2021-GASGAS-MC-450F,7210) — GasGas MC 450F 2021, Vital MX 2020-12-22

- suspension: Ohlins RXF 48 fork + TTX Flow shock — Suspension; found: Called the biggest change to the bike; $4,275
- exhaust: Pro Circuit Ti-6 Pro — Exhaust; found: $1,049.96
- clutch_cover: Rekluse Billet clutch cover — Weight; found: Much lighter than the heavy stock cover; $169
- bar_mounts: Xtrig PHDS bar mounts — Vibration; found: $198.44
- handlebar: ODI Bars and grips — Ergonomics
- footpegs: Scar Footpegs — Footpegs; found: $309.28
- wheels: Haan / Dubya Haan hubs, Dubya-built wheels — Wheels
- seat: Guts Seat cover — Grip; found: Keeps the rider secure
- drivetrain: DDC Sprocket — Drive
- hardware: Mettech / Works Connection Ti triple-tree bolts and front axle; axle kit — Weight/finish
- ecu: Carson City Motorsports Mapping service — Power

### [2024 KTM 300 SX Garage Build (Keefer)](https://www.keeferinctesting.com/2024-ktm-300-sx-garage-build/) — KTM 300 SX 2024, Keefer Inc Testing 2026-05-20

## Gaps and work not done

- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Husqvarna 2026 FC/FX missing from several fitment lists (Raptor, P3) while 2027 listed - likely retailer data gaps; treat 2026 Husqvarna fitment as needing confirmation.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) XC/XC-F 2023 chassis membership inferred from fitment lists, not from a KTM press statement.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Neken handlebar (stock on 2027 KTM) bends/SKUs, Renthal bars, grips, bar mounts not researched.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Galfer manufacturer part numbers and galferusa fitment page not reached; Braking, Brembo upgrades, EBC/Galfer pads, master cylinders not researched.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) GasGas 2024+ Braktec brake compatibility for oversize rotor kits and Brembo-pattern levers unresolved (sources contradict).
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Linkages / pull rods for KTM 2023+ not found in this pass.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Haan wheels (Sprocket Center custom KTM set) - no fitment, SKU or price captured. Warp 9, Talon, DID wheels, rim locks, spokes not researched.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Cycra and Acerbis handguards: Cycra listing had no fitment/SKU; KTM mount kits not identified.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Disc guards, frame guards, pipe guards, Enduro Engineering skid plates not researched.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Chains (DID, Regina, EK) and Renthal/JT/Talon sprockets not researched.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Works Connection Pro Launch for KTM 2023+/GasGas 2024+ not found (KTM 2023+ has OEM holeshot); WC Elite perches, brake pedals, shift levers not researched.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) KTM 85 SX 2025+ aftermarket fitment (20 mm rear axle) not researched; Husqvarna TC 85 / GasGas MC 85 2025 platform sharing unconfirmed.
- (ktm-chassis-controls-wheels.json) Luxon and Ride Engineering 2023+ part numbers/prices not captured (RMATV page blocked by bot protection).

- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) JE, CP, Athena (2023+), Pro X 450 2023+ pistons and any high-compression or big-bore kits for 2023+ 4T engines not found.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) Hot Cams/Web Cams for 2023+ 4T engines not found; Kibblewhite/Del West valves and springs not found for any in-scope year.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) Crank/rod kits (Hot Rods, Pro X) for 2020+ in-scope models not found.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) Cometic/Athena gasket kits not researched.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) Hinson part numbers/fitment unreliable (retailer data corrupted; Cycle Gear marks discontinued). Hinson site not checked.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) Rekluse: manufacturer site rekluse.com fetch was blocked (provenance), so no high-confidence SKUs. RadiusX/auto clutches, Core EXP not covered. Barnett springs/plates, clutch slave/hydraulics, KTM DDS notes not covered.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) Magnetic drain plugs, stators, 4T flywheel weights not found.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) Mishimoto: no KTM motocross radiator listing found. CV4: no 2023+ KTM listing found (X-Grip and Moose/Samco used instead). Samco Sport direct SKUs not found.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) Boyesen 2T items (water pump, ignition cover, reed/power valve) for 2023+ TBI not found; Boyesen 4T clutch cover has no MPN.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) KTM PowerParts fan kit and flywheel options not checked.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) 85 SX essentially not covered.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) 2026 and 2027 model-year changes: only confirmed that 2027 SX-F is 'largely unchanged' with unspecified sound-related updates; no 2026/2027 2T changes checked.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) No rider feedback found for any 2023+ engine/clutch/cooling aftermarket part; only an MXA stock 300 SX test, a Vital forum thread, and a 2013 MXA test of an older Steahly part.
- (ktm-engine-clutch-cooling.json) All confidence is medium or unverified; no manufacturer page lists both SKU and fitment, except Athena's page which has fitment but no SKU.
- (ktm-exhaust-intake-fuel.json) FMF part numbers for 2023+ KTM-group 4-strokes (Factory 4.1 RCT 250/350/450 full and slip-on) not found; fmfracing.com search URL was blocked (provenance), and retailers mostly list 2019-2023 SKUs.

- (ktm-exhaust-intake-fuel.json) Pro Circuit 2-stroke pipes for 2023+ TBI: searches returned only older years.
- (ktm-exhaust-intake-fuel.json) Pro Circuit suffix meaning (FP vs BC) is inferred, not confirmed; 2026 Husqvarna FC fitment missing from Cycle Gear lists (likely data gap).
- (ktm-exhaust-intake-fuel.json) MY26 Factory Edition / MY27 standard SX-F revised airbox and filter: details not found; aftermarket filter and exhaust compatibility needs checking.
- (ktm-exhaust-intake-fuel.json) 2027 125 SX / TC125 EFI: throttle body size and aftermarket support (pipes, ECU) unknown; a press report says TC125 is TBI but TBI vs other EFI layout on KTM 125 is unconfirmed.
- (ktm-exhaust-intake-fuel.json) 2025+ new-generation 85 SX: no aftermarket pipe/silencer confirmed; FMF 025222 is listed for 2018-2024 only. PWK28 jetting parts not researched.
